# Retrieval evaluation

Runs: `bm25_dev_k1-1.2_b-0.75`, `bm25_dev_k1-0.6_b-0.3`, `bm25_dev_k1-0.6_b-0.5`, `bm25_dev_k1-0.6_b-0.75`, `bm25_dev_k1-0.6_b-0.9`, `bm25_dev_k1-0.9_b-0.3`, `bm25_dev_k1-0.9_b-0.5`, `bm25_dev_k1-0.9_b-0.75`, `bm25_dev_k1-0.9_b-0.9`, `bm25_dev_k1-1.2_b-0.3`, `bm25_dev_k1-1.2_b-0.5`, `bm25_dev_k1-1.2_b-0.9`, `bm25_dev_k1-1.5_b-0.3`, `bm25_dev_k1-1.5_b-0.5`, `bm25_dev_k1-1.5_b-0.75`, `bm25_dev_k1-1.5_b-0.9`, `bm25_dev_k1-2_b-0.3`, `bm25_dev_k1-2_b-0.5`, `bm25_dev_k1-2_b-0.75`, `bm25_dev_k1-2_b-0.9` | split: dev | cells: mean [95% bootstrap CI, 1000 resamples]

Gold levels: **strict** = own disproof; **lenient** = own or near-duplicate disproof; **case** = disproof or claim of own / near-duplicate case; **proxy** = same-debunk trustworthy article

Rows: all queries, then by title–disproof word overlap (`overlap_bin`) and by entity type (`entity_bin`: entity-driven = the query's specific named entities occur in the gold evidence; formulation-driven = no specific entity, or the evidence does not repeat it). The two groupings overlap; each sums to all.
